    struct Frame {
      viewProj: mat4x4f,   // camera-relative view-projection (reverse-Z, infinite)

      windP:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // [0].xy = direction, [0].z = m/s; [1].x = HDR storage divisor

      fogExt:   vec4f,     // rgb extinction per metre near sea level

      absorption: vec4f, // RGB proxy wavelengths 650/550/450 nm, 1/m; water height at camera
      backscatter: vec4f, // particulate + molecular backscatter, 1/m; dt
      moonDir: vec4f,     // direction; illuminated fraction
      solarDir: vec4f,    // astronomical sun; night blend
      weather: vec4f,     // rain, spray, cloud type, cloud thickness km
      control: vec4f,     // cloud samples, light samples, underwater mode, star rotation
      celestial: vec4f,   // lunar irradiance, lunar angular radius, Lambert phase, exposure adaptation
      worldOrigin: vec4f, // global xz kilometres; this frame's local-origin shift xz metres
      waveOrigins: array<vec4f,3>, // two modulo cascade UV origins per vec4
      patternOrigins: array<vec4f,2>, // active/residual foam; streak/bubble UV origins
    };
    
    const PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979;
    const RG: f32 = 6360.0;
    const RT: f32 = 6460.0;
    const RAY_S: vec3f = vec3f(5.802e-3, 13.558e-3, 33.1e-3);
    const MIE_S: f32 = 3.996e-3;
    const MIE_E: f32 = 4.40e-3;
    const OZO_A: vec3f = vec3f(0.650e-3, 1.881e-3, 0.085e-3);
    const SUN_ANG_R: f32 = 0.004675;
    const TRANS_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const TRANS_H: f32 = 64.0;
    const MS_SIZE: f32 = 32.0;
    
    struct Medium { ext: vec3f, rayS: vec3f, mieS: f32 };
    
    fn medium(h: f32, haze: f32) -> Medium {
      let hh = max(h, 0.0);
      let dR = exp(-hh / 8.0);
      // aerosols: regular 1.2 km layer plus a dense marine boundary layer
      let dM = exp(-hh / 1.2);
      let dO = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(hh - 25.0) / 15.0);
      var m: Medium;
      m.rayS = RAY_S * dR;
      m.mieS = MIE_S * dM * haze;
      m.ext = m.rayS + vec3f(MIE_E * dM * haze) + OZO_A * dO;
      return m;
    }
    
    fn raySphere(ro: vec3f, rd: vec3f, R: f32) -> vec2f {
      let l = length(ro);
      let b = dot(ro, rd);
      let c = (l - R) * (l + R);
      let d = b * b - c;
      if (d < 0.0) { return vec2f(-1.0, -1.0); }
      let s = sqrt(d);
      return vec2f(-b - s, -b + s);
    }
    
    fn rhoOf(r: f32) -> f32 { return sqrt(max(0.0, (r - RG) * (r + RG))); }
    
    fn transRMuToUV(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = rhoOf(r);
      let disc = r * r * (mu * mu - 1.0) + RT * RT;
      let d = max(0.0, -r * mu + sqrt(max(disc, 0.0)));
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let x = (d - dMin) / (dMax - dMin);
      let y = rho / H;
      return vec2f(x, y);
    }
    
    fn transUVToRMu(uv: vec2f) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = H * uv.y;
      let r = sqrt(rho * rho + RG * RG);
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let d = dMin + uv.x * (dMax - dMin);
      var mu = 1.0;
      if (d > 0.0) { mu = (H * H - rho * rho - d * d) / (2.0 * r * d); }
      return vec2f(r, clamp(mu, -1.0, 1.0));
    }
    
    fn fromUnitToSubUvs(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u + 0.5 / res) * (res / (res + 1.0)); }
    fn fromSubUvsToUnit(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u - 0.5 / res) * (res / (res - 1.0)); }
    
    fn phaseRayleigh(c: f32) -> f32 { return 3.0 / (16.0 * PI) * (1.0 + c * c); }
    fn phaseMie(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return 3.0 / (8.0 * PI) * ((1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + c * c)) / ((2.0 + g2) * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    fn phaseHG(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * PI * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    
    // true when the sun is below the geometric horizon of a point at radius r
    fn sunBelowHorizon(r: f32, mu: f32) -> bool {
      return mu < -rhoOf(r) / r;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> F:Frame;
    @group(0) @binding(1) var transLUT:texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(2) var skyLUT:texture_2d_array<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(3) var sampC:sampler;
    @group(0) @binding(4) var dst:texture_storage_2d<rgba16float,write>;
    
    fn pcgHash(v: u32) -> u32 {
      let s = v * 747796405u + 2891336453u;
      let w = ((s >> ((s >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ s) * 277803737u;
      return (w >> 22u) ^ w;
    }
    fn hashGrad(i: vec2i) -> vec2f {
      let h = pcgHash(u32(i.x) * 1664525u + pcgHash(u32(i.y) + 1013904223u));
      let a = f32(h) * (6.28318530718 / 4294967296.0);
      return vec2f(cos(a), sin(a));
    }
    fn gnoise(p: vec2f) -> f32 {
      let i = vec2i(floor(p));
      let f = p - floor(p);
      let u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);
      let a = dot(hashGrad(i), f);
      let b = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 0)), f - vec2f(1.0, 0.0));
      let c = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(0, 1)), f - vec2f(0.0, 1.0));
      let d = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 1)), f - vec2f(1.0, 1.0));
      return mix(mix(a, b, u.x), mix(c, d, u.x), u.y);
    }
    
    const SKY_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const SKY_H: f32 = 144.0;
    
    fn camR() -> f32 { return RG + F.atmo.y; }
    
    fn sampleTrans(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec3f {
      return textureSampleLevel(transLUT, sampC, transRMuToUV(r, mu), 0.0).rgb;
    }
    
    fn sunIlluminance() -> vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.sunDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(), F.sunDir.y) * F.sunDir.w;
    }
    fn moonIlluminance()->vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.moonDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(),F.moonDir.y)*F.celestial.x;
    }
    
    fn skyViewUV(dir: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> vec2f {
      let r = camR();
      let vHorizon = rhoOf(r);
      let cosBeta = vHorizon / r;
      let beta = acos(clamp(cosBeta, -1.0, 1.0));
      let zha = PI - beta;
      let vzc = clamp(dir.y, -1.0, 1.0);
      var uv: vec2f;
      if (vzc >= -cosBeta) {
        var coord = acos(vzc) / zha;
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        uv.y = coord * 0.5;
      } else {
        var coord = (acos(vzc) - zha) / beta;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        uv.y = coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
      }
      let dl = length(dir.xz);
      let sl = length(light.xz);
      var lvc = 1.0;
      if (dl > 1e-6 && sl > 1e-6) { lvc = clamp(dot(dir.xz, light.xz) / (dl * sl), -1.0, 1.0); }
      uv.x = sqrt(max(-lvc * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0));
      return vec2f(fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.x, SKY_W), fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.y, SKY_H));
    }
    
    fn skyAtmo(dir: vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let solar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0u,0.0).rgb*F.sunDir.w;
      let lunar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),1u,0.0).rgb*F.celestial.x;
      return solar+lunar;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(5) var cloudNoise:texture_3d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(6) var cloudNoiseSampler:sampler;
    fn hash3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      var q=fract(p*0.1031);
      q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);
      return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);
    }
    fn noise3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      let i=floor(p); let f=fract(p); let u=f*f*(3.0-2.0*f);
      return mix(mix(mix(hash3(i),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,0)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,0)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,0)),u.x),u.y),
        mix(mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,0,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,1)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,1)),u.x),u.y),u.z);
    }
    fn filteredNoise(p:vec3f, footprint:f32)->f32 {
      return mix(noise3(p),0.5,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint));
    }
    fn thermalShape(world:vec3f,altitude:f32,coverage:f32,storm:f32)->f32 {
      let size=mix(3.6,7.0,storm);
      let cell=floor(world.xz/size);
      var field=-1.0;
      for(var j=-1;j<=1;j++){for(var i=-1;i<=1;i++){
        let id=cell+vec2f(f32(i),f32(j));
        let random=vec3f(hash3(vec3f(id,13.7)),hash3(vec3f(id.yx,29.3)),hash3(vec3f(id,71.9)));
        let strength=smoothstep(random.z-0.12,random.z+0.12,coverage);
        if(strength<0.01){continue;}
        let centre=(id+0.2+random.xy*0.6)*size;
        let depth=F.weather.w*(0.20+0.64*random.y*random.y);
        let bottom=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.015+0.08*random.x);
        let h=(altitude-bottom)/max(depth,0.15);
        let radius=size*(0.17+0.29*random.x)*pow(strength,0.333333);
        let bend=F.windP[0].xy*h*h*depth*0.18;
        let q=vec3f((world.xz-centre-bend)/radius,(h-0.30)/0.52);
        let core=1.0-length(q);
        let turret=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy-(random.xy-0.5)*0.95)/(0.44+random.x*0.25),(h-0.52)/0.44));
        let side=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy+(random.yx-0.5)*1.4)/0.62,(h-0.31)/0.39));
        let lobe=max(max(core,turret),side);
        let base=smoothstep(-0.01,0.055,h);
        field=max(field,mix(-1.0,lobe,base));
      }}
      return field;
    }
    fn cloudDensityFiltered(p: vec3f, detail: bool, footprint:f32) -> f32 {
      let altitude=p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x || altitude>F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w){return 0.0;}
      let drift=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0.0,F.windP[0].y)*F.cloudP.w*F.windP[0].z*0.00065;
      let world=p+vec3f(F.camPos.x*0.001+F.worldOrigin.x,0,F.camPos.z*0.001+F.worldOrigin.y)-drift;
      let strat=select(0.0,1.0,F.weather.z>0.5 && F.weather.z<1.5);
      let storm=step(1.5,F.weather.z);
      // Separate mesoscale organisation from billows. Local depth and cloud base
      // vary across each bank instead of sharing one repeated horizontal profile.
      let weather=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.008,0.31,world.z*0.008),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*1.024),0.0));
      let cell=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.047,0.67,world.z*0.047),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*6.016),0.0));
      let cover=clamp(F.cloudP.y+(weather.a-0.5)*3.2+(cell.a-0.5)*0.65,0.0,1.0);
      let baseHeight=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.025+0.14*cell.a);
      let depth=F.weather.w*mix(0.48+0.48*weather.r,0.22+0.15*cell.r,strat);
      let h=(altitude-baseHeight)/max(depth,0.1);
      if((strat>0.5 && (h<0.0 || h>1.0)) || cover<0.001){return 0.0;}
      let profile=smoothstep(0.0,0.075,h)*(1.0-smoothstep(mix(0.6,0.7,storm),1.0,h));
      let scale=vec3f(mix(0.62,0.24,storm),mix(0.60,0.42,strat),mix(0.62,0.24,storm));
      let shear=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0,F.windP[0].y)*(h*h*0.13*F.weather.w);
      let q=(world+shear)*scale;
      let lod=max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*max(scale.x,scale.y)*128.0),0.0);
      let shape=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q,lod);
      let base=clamp((shape.r-0.5)*2.8+0.68-(1.0-shape.g)*0.12,0.0,1.0);
      let probability=cover*profile;
      var density=clamp((base-(1.0-probability))/max(probability,0.05),0.0,1.0);
      if(strat<0.5){
        let thermal=thermalShape(world,altitude,cover,storm);
        let billow=(shape.r-0.5)*3.4+(shape.g-0.4)*0.65;
        density=clamp((thermal+billow)*2.0,0.0,1.0)*smoothstep(-0.32,0.06,thermal);
        // Storm banks merge into a connected lower deck as coverage approaches one.
        let deck=clamp((base-(1.0-cover*profile))*2.6,0.0,1.0);
        density=max(density,deck*storm*smoothstep(0.65,0.96,cover));
      }
      if(density>0.0){
        let erosion=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q*2.83+vec3f(0.17,0.31,0.59),lod+1.5).b;
        let carve=(1.0-erosion)*mix(0.42,0.07,strat)*mix(1.3,0.65,smoothstep(0.0,0.3,h));
        density=clamp((density-carve)/(1.0-carve),0.0,1.0);
      }
      return density;
    }
    fn cloudDensity(p:vec3f,detail:bool)->f32{return cloudDensityFiltered(p,detail,0.0);}
    fn cloudShadowFor(p: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> f32 {
      if(light.y<0.01){return 1.0;}
      let start=max(0.0,(F.cloudP.x-p.y)/light.y);
      let lengthRay=min(30.0,F.weather.w/max(light.y,0.03));
      var od=0.0;
      for(var j=0u;j<12u;j++) {
        od+=cloudDensity(p+light*(start+(f32(j)+0.5)*lengthRay/12.0),true)*lengthRay/12.0;
      }
      return exp(-od*F.cloudP.z*6.0);
    }
    fn cloudShadowAt(p:vec3f)->f32 {return cloudShadowFor(p,F.sunDir.xyz);}
    fn cloudLayers(dir: vec3f,pixAng:f32,aa:vec2f,sunT:ptr<function,f32>) -> vec4f {
      *sunT=1.0;
      if(F.cloudP.y<0.001 && F.atmo.z<0.001){return vec4f(0);}
      let altitude=max(F.camPos.y*0.001,0.001);
      if(dir.y < -sqrt(2.0*altitude/RG)-0.002){return vec4f(0);}
      let ro=vec3f(0,altitude,0);
      let sphereOrigin=vec3f(0,RG+altitude,0);
      let outer=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w);
      let inner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x);
      var start=max(0.0,outer.x);
      var finish=max(0.0,outer.y);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x){start=max(0.0,inner.y);}
      else if(inner.x>0.0){finish=inner.x;}
      let ground=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG);
      if(ground.x>0.0){finish=min(finish,ground.x);}
      finish=min(finish,200.0);
      // Keep the quadrature topology continuous across image rows. Rounding a
      // view-angle-dependent count rephases every sample at each integer boundary.
      let samples=u32(F.control.x*1.5);
      let logRange=log(max((finish+1.0)/(start+1.0),1.0));
      var trans=1.0;var radiance=vec3f(0);
      let ambient=skyAtmo(vec3f(0,1,0))*0.65+skyAtmo(normalize(vec3f(dir.x,0.25,dir.z)))*0.22;
      let phase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-0.25);
      // Deterministic segment quadrature: no screen-space jitter to crawl in motion.
      // Exponential spacing preserves nearby volume detail and reaches the curved horizon.
      for(var i=0u;i<samples && logRange>0.0;i++) {
        let a=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let b=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i+1u)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let ds=b-a;
        let t=(a+b)*0.5;
        let p=ro+dir*t;
        let footprint=max(ds*0.14,t*pixAng);
        var density=0.0;
        // Four subsegments retained after the cheaper two-point experiment visibly
        // reintroduced distant density slices. No jitter or temporal accumulation.
        for(var sub=0u;sub<4u;sub++){
          density+=cloudDensityFiltered(ro+dir*(a+ds*(f32(sub)+0.5)*0.25),true,footprint)*0.25;
        }
        if(density>0.001) {
          let radial=vec3f(p.x,RG+p.y,p.z);
          let radius=length(radial);let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
          var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
          if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
          var shadow=0.0;
          var lightStep=0.06;
          var lightDist=0.03;
          for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++) {
            shadow+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.sunDir.xyz*lightDist,false,lightStep*0.35)*lightStep;
            lightDist+=lightStep;lightStep*=2.1;
          }
          let sigma=F.cloudP.z*6.0;
          let optical=shadow*sigma;
          let direct=exp(-optical)*phase;
          let multi=(0.6*exp(-optical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-optical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
          let height=clamp((p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG)-F.cloudP.x)/F.weather.w,0.0,1.0);
          var lunar=vec3f(0);
          if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
            let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
            if(!sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu)){
              let moonPhase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),-0.25);
              var moonOptical=0.0;var moonStep=0.06;var moonDist=0.03;
              for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++){
                moonOptical+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.moonDir.xyz*moonDist,false,moonStep*0.35)*moonStep;
                moonDist+=moonStep;moonStep*=2.1;
              }
              moonOptical*=sigma;
              let moonMulti=(0.6*exp(-moonOptical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-moonOptical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
              lunar=sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x*(exp(-moonOptical)*moonPhase+moonMulti);
            }
          }
          let light=ambient*mix(0.18,1.0,sqrt(height))+sun*(direct+multi)+lunar;
          let stepT=exp(-density*sigma*ds);
          let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
          let fog=skyAtmo(dir);
          radiance+=trans*(1.0-stepT)*(light*aerial+fog*(1.0-aerial));
          trans*=stepT;
          if(trans<0.005){break;}
        }
      }
      // Spherical ice shell: finite grazing path, independent of the lower cloud cutoff.
      let iceInner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.0);
      let iceOuter=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.3);
      var iceStart=max(0.0,iceOuter.x);
      var iceFinish=max(0.0,iceOuter.y);
      if(altitude<7.0){iceStart=max(0.0,iceInner.y);}
      else if(iceInner.x>0.0){iceFinish=iceInner.x;}
      if(ground.x>0.0){iceFinish=min(iceFinish,ground.x);}
      if(iceFinish>iceStart && F.atmo.z>0.001) {
        let t=(iceStart+iceFinish)*0.5;
        let p=(ro+dir*t).xz+F.camPos.xz*0.001+F.worldOrigin.xy;
        let warp=vec2f(gnoise(p*0.14),gnoise(p*0.21+vec2f(8.3,1.1)))*2.2;
        let footprint=t*pixAng;
        let low=mix(gnoise((p+warp)*vec2f(0.22,0.85)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*0.85));
        let high=mix(gnoise(p*vec2f(0.63,2.3)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*2.3));
        let ice=max(0.0,0.65*low+0.35*high+F.atmo.z-0.45);
        let opacity=1.0-exp(-ice*0.6*(iceFinish-iceStart));
        let radial=sphereOrigin+dir*t;let radius=length(radial);
        let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
        var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
        if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
        let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
        let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
        let moon=select(sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x,vec3f(0),sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu));
        let iceLight=mix(skyAtmo(dir),ambient+(sun+moon)*0.12,aerial);
        radiance+=trans*opacity*iceLight;
        trans*=1.0-opacity;
      }
      *sunT=trans;
      return vec4f(radiance,1.0-trans);
    }
    fn nightStars(dir:vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let q=vec2f(atan2(dir.x,dir.z)+F.control.w,asin(clamp(dir.y,-1.0,1.0)))*650.0;
      let cell=floor(q);let offset=fract(q)-0.5;
      let h=hash3(vec3f(cell,17.0));
      let stars=smoothstep(0.995,1.0,h)*exp(-dot(offset,offset)*70.0);
      return vec3f(0.65,0.78,1.0)*stars*0.018*F.solarDir.w*smoothstep(0.0,0.2,dir.y);
    }
    fn sunDisk(dir:vec3f)->vec3f {
      let angle=acos(clamp(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-1.0,1.0));
      let pix=max(F.screen.w*length(F.camUp.xyz),0.00002);
      let disk=1.0-smoothstep(SUN_ANG_R-pix,SUN_ANG_R+pix,angle);
      let vis=step(-0.006,dir.y);
      var radiance=disk*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.sunDir.w/(PI*SUN_ANG_R*SUN_ANG_R);
      if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
        let radius=F.celestial.y;
        let tangent=dir-F.moonDir.xyz*dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz);
        let r2=dot(tangent,tangent)/(radius*radius);
        let moonMask=1.0-smoothstep(radius-pix,radius+pix,length(tangent));
        let normal=normalize(tangent/radius-F.moonDir.xyz*sqrt(max(0.0,1.0-r2)));
        let illumination=max(dot(normal,F.solarDir.xyz),0.0);
        let normalization=max((2.0/3.0)*F.celestial.z,1e-7);
        radiance+=moonMask*step(0.0,dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz))*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.celestial.x*illumination/(PI*radius*radius*normalization);
      }
      return radiance;
    }
    fn skyRadiance(dirIn:vec3f,withSun:bool,pixAng:f32)->vec3f {
      let dir=normalize(dirIn);
      var tr=1.0;
      let cl=cloudLayers(dir,pixAng,vec2f(0),&tr);
      var L=skyAtmo(dir)*(1.0-cl.a)+cl.rgb+nightStars(dir)*tr;
      if(withSun){L+=sunDisk(dir)*tr;}
      return L;
    }
    
    @compute @workgroup_size(8,8) fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3u) {
     let dims=textureDimensions(dst);if(any(id.xy>=dims)){return;}
     let uv=(vec2f(id.xy)+0.5)/vec2f(dims);
     let ndc=vec2f(uv.x*2.0-1.0,1.0-uv.y*2.0);
     let dir=normalize(F.camFwd.xyz+ndc.x*F.camRight.xyz+ndc.y*F.camUp.xyz);
     var tr=1.0;let cl=cloudLayers(dir,2.0*length(F.camUp.xyz)/f32(dims.y),vec2f(0),&tr);
     textureStore(dst,id.xy,cl);
    }
    
    struct Frame {
      viewProj: mat4x4f,   // camera-relative view-projection (reverse-Z, infinite)
      camPos:   vec4f,     // xyz metres, w = time (s)
      camRight: vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovX/2)
      camUp:    vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovY/2)
      camFwd:   vec4f,     // xyz
      sunDir:   vec4f,     // xyz towards sun, w = solar illuminance scale
      screen:   vec4f,     // w, h, 1/w, 1/h
      cascL:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // cascade patch sizes (m), index [c>>2][c&3]
      cascInvL: array<vec4f, 2>,
      windP:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // [0].xy = direction, [0].z = m/s; [1].x = HDR storage divisor
      lodP:     vec4f,     // CDLOD base range, grid quads per node, -, -
      post:     vec4f,     // exposure, bloom strength, frame index, -
      atmo:     vec4f,     // haze (mie multiplier), camera altitude km, -, -
      waterCol: vec4f,     // body scatter colour rgb, unresolved slope variance
      sssCol:   vec4f,     // sss colour rgb, sss strength
      foamP:    vec4f,     // J threshold, J gain, foam intensity, lace sharpness
      fogExt:   vec4f,     // rgb extinction per metre near sea level
      cloudP:   vec4f,     // altitude km, coverage, optical density, wind time
      absorption: vec4f, // RGB proxy wavelengths 650/550/450 nm, 1/m; water height at camera
      backscatter: vec4f, // particulate + molecular backscatter, 1/m; dt
      moonDir: vec4f,     // direction; illuminated fraction
      solarDir: vec4f,    // astronomical sun; night blend
      weather: vec4f,     // rain, spray, cloud type, cloud thickness km
      control: vec4f,     // cloud samples, light samples, underwater mode, star rotation
      celestial: vec4f,   // lunar irradiance, lunar angular radius, Lambert phase, exposure adaptation
      worldOrigin: vec4f, // global xz kilometres; this frame's local-origin shift xz metres
      waveOrigins: array<vec4f,3>, // two modulo cascade UV origins per vec4
      patternOrigins: array<vec4f,2>, // active/residual foam; streak/bubble UV origins
    };
    
    const PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979;
    const RG: f32 = 6360.0;
    const RT: f32 = 6460.0;
    const RAY_S: vec3f = vec3f(5.802e-3, 13.558e-3, 33.1e-3);
    const MIE_S: f32 = 3.996e-3;
    const MIE_E: f32 = 4.40e-3;
    const OZO_A: vec3f = vec3f(0.650e-3, 1.881e-3, 0.085e-3);
    const SUN_ANG_R: f32 = 0.004675;
    const TRANS_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const TRANS_H: f32 = 64.0;
    const MS_SIZE: f32 = 32.0;
    
    struct Medium { ext: vec3f, rayS: vec3f, mieS: f32 };
    
    fn medium(h: f32, haze: f32) -> Medium {
      let hh = max(h, 0.0);
      let dR = exp(-hh / 8.0);
      // aerosols: regular 1.2 km layer plus a dense marine boundary layer
      let dM = exp(-hh / 1.2);
      let dO = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(hh - 25.0) / 15.0);
      var m: Medium;
      m.rayS = RAY_S * dR;
      m.mieS = MIE_S * dM * haze;
      m.ext = m.rayS + vec3f(MIE_E * dM * haze) + OZO_A * dO;
      return m;
    }
    
    fn raySphere(ro: vec3f, rd: vec3f, R: f32) -> vec2f {
      let l = length(ro);
      let b = dot(ro, rd);
      let c = (l - R) * (l + R);
      let d = b * b - c;
      if (d < 0.0) { return vec2f(-1.0, -1.0); }
      let s = sqrt(d);
      return vec2f(-b - s, -b + s);
    }
    
    fn rhoOf(r: f32) -> f32 { return sqrt(max(0.0, (r - RG) * (r + RG))); }
    
    fn transRMuToUV(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = rhoOf(r);
      let disc = r * r * (mu * mu - 1.0) + RT * RT;
      let d = max(0.0, -r * mu + sqrt(max(disc, 0.0)));
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let x = (d - dMin) / (dMax - dMin);
      let y = rho / H;
      return vec2f(x, y);
    }
    
    fn transUVToRMu(uv: vec2f) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = H * uv.y;
      let r = sqrt(rho * rho + RG * RG);
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let d = dMin + uv.x * (dMax - dMin);
      var mu = 1.0;
      if (d > 0.0) { mu = (H * H - rho * rho - d * d) / (2.0 * r * d); }
      return vec2f(r, clamp(mu, -1.0, 1.0));
    }
    
    fn fromUnitToSubUvs(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u + 0.5 / res) * (res / (res + 1.0)); }
    fn fromSubUvsToUnit(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u - 0.5 / res) * (res / (res - 1.0)); }
    
    fn phaseRayleigh(c: f32) -> f32 { return 3.0 / (16.0 * PI) * (1.0 + c * c); }
    fn phaseMie(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return 3.0 / (8.0 * PI) * ((1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + c * c)) / ((2.0 + g2) * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    fn phaseHG(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * PI * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    
    // true when the sun is below the geometric horizon of a point at radius r
    fn sunBelowHorizon(r: f32, mu: f32) -> bool {
      return mu < -rhoOf(r) / r;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> F:Frame;
    @group(0) @binding(1) var transLUT:texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(2) var skyLUT:texture_2d_array<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(3) var sampC:sampler;
    @group(0) @binding(4) var dst:texture_storage_2d<rgba16float,write>;
    
    fn pcgHash(v: u32) -> u32 {
      let s = v * 747796405u + 2891336453u;
      let w = ((s >> ((s >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ s) * 277803737u;
      return (w >> 22u) ^ w;
    }
    fn hashGrad(i: vec2i) -> vec2f {
      let h = pcgHash(u32(i.x) * 1664525u + pcgHash(u32(i.y) + 1013904223u));
      let a = f32(h) * (6.28318530718 / 4294967296.0);
      return vec2f(cos(a), sin(a));
    }
    fn gnoise(p: vec2f) -> f32 {
      let i = vec2i(floor(p));
      let f = p - floor(p);
      let u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);
      let a = dot(hashGrad(i), f);
      let b = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 0)), f - vec2f(1.0, 0.0));
      let c = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(0, 1)), f - vec2f(0.0, 1.0));
      let d = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 1)), f - vec2f(1.0, 1.0));
      return mix(mix(a, b, u.x), mix(c, d, u.x), u.y);
    }
    
    const SKY_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const SKY_H: f32 = 144.0;
    
    fn camR() -> f32 { return RG + F.atmo.y; }
    
    fn sampleTrans(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec3f {
      return textureSampleLevel(transLUT, sampC, transRMuToUV(r, mu), 0.0).rgb;
    }
    
    fn sunIlluminance() -> vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.sunDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(), F.sunDir.y) * F.sunDir.w;
    }
    fn moonIlluminance()->vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.moonDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(),F.moonDir.y)*F.celestial.x;
    }
    
    fn skyViewUV(dir: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> vec2f {
      let r = camR();
      let vHorizon = rhoOf(r);
      let cosBeta = vHorizon / r;
      let beta = acos(clamp(cosBeta, -1.0, 1.0));
      let zha = PI - beta;
      let vzc = clamp(dir.y, -1.0, 1.0);
      var uv: vec2f;
      if (vzc >= -cosBeta) {
        var coord = acos(vzc) / zha;
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        uv.y = coord * 0.5;
      } else {
        var coord = (acos(vzc) - zha) / beta;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        uv.y = coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
      }
      let dl = length(dir.xz);
      let sl = length(light.xz);
      var lvc = 1.0;
      if (dl > 1e-6 && sl > 1e-6) { lvc = clamp(dot(dir.xz, light.xz) / (dl * sl), -1.0, 1.0); }
      uv.x = sqrt(max(-lvc * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0));
      return vec2f(fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.x, SKY_W), fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.y, SKY_H));
    }
    
    fn skyAtmo(dir: vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let solar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0u,0.0).rgb*F.sunDir.w;
      let lunar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),1u,0.0).rgb*F.celestial.x;
      return solar+lunar;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(5) var cloudNoise:texture_3d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(6) var cloudNoiseSampler:sampler;
    fn hash3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      var q=fract(p*0.1031);
      q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);
      return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);
    }
    fn noise3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      let i=floor(p); let f=fract(p); let u=f*f*(3.0-2.0*f);
      return mix(mix(mix(hash3(i),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,0)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,0)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,0)),u.x),u.y),
        mix(mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,0,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,1)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,1)),u.x),u.y),u.z);
    }
    fn filteredNoise(p:vec3f, footprint:f32)->f32 {
      return mix(noise3(p),0.5,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint));
    }
    fn thermalShape(world:vec3f,altitude:f32,coverage:f32,storm:f32)->f32 {
      let size=mix(3.6,7.0,storm);
      let cell=floor(world.xz/size);
      var field=-1.0;
      for(var j=-1;j<=1;j++){for(var i=-1;i<=1;i++){
        let id=cell+vec2f(f32(i),f32(j));
        let random=vec3f(hash3(vec3f(id,13.7)),hash3(vec3f(id.yx,29.3)),hash3(vec3f(id,71.9)));
        let strength=smoothstep(random.z-0.12,random.z+0.12,coverage);
        if(strength<0.01){continue;}
        let centre=(id+0.2+random.xy*0.6)*size;
        let depth=F.weather.w*(0.20+0.64*random.y*random.y);
        let bottom=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.015+0.08*random.x);
        let h=(altitude-bottom)/max(depth,0.15);
        let radius=size*(0.17+0.29*random.x)*pow(strength,0.333333);
        let bend=F.windP[0].xy*h*h*depth*0.18;
        let q=vec3f((world.xz-centre-bend)/radius,(h-0.30)/0.52);
        let core=1.0-length(q);
        let turret=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy-(random.xy-0.5)*0.95)/(0.44+random.x*0.25),(h-0.52)/0.44));
        let side=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy+(random.yx-0.5)*1.4)/0.62,(h-0.31)/0.39));
        let lobe=max(max(core,turret),side);
        let base=smoothstep(-0.01,0.055,h);
        field=max(field,mix(-1.0,lobe,base));
      }}
      return field;
    }
    fn cloudDensityFiltered(p: vec3f, detail: bool, footprint:f32) -> f32 {
      let altitude=p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x || altitude>F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w){return 0.0;}
      let drift=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0.0,F.windP[0].y)*F.cloudP.w*F.windP[0].z*0.00065;
      let world=p+vec3f(F.camPos.x*0.001+F.worldOrigin.x,0,F.camPos.z*0.001+F.worldOrigin.y)-drift;
      let strat=select(0.0,1.0,F.weather.z>0.5 && F.weather.z<1.5);
      let storm=step(1.5,F.weather.z);
      // Separate mesoscale organisation from billows. Local depth and cloud base
      // vary across each bank instead of sharing one repeated horizontal profile.
      let weather=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.008,0.31,world.z*0.008),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*1.024),0.0));
      let cell=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.047,0.67,world.z*0.047),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*6.016),0.0));
      let cover=clamp(F.cloudP.y+(weather.a-0.5)*3.2+(cell.a-0.5)*0.65,0.0,1.0);
      let baseHeight=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.025+0.14*cell.a);
      let depth=F.weather.w*mix(0.48+0.48*weather.r,0.22+0.15*cell.r,strat);
      let h=(altitude-baseHeight)/max(depth,0.1);
      if((strat>0.5 && (h<0.0 || h>1.0)) || cover<0.001){return 0.0;}
      let profile=smoothstep(0.0,0.075,h)*(1.0-smoothstep(mix(0.6,0.7,storm),1.0,h));
      let scale=vec3f(mix(0.62,0.24,storm),mix(0.60,0.42,strat),mix(0.62,0.24,storm));
      let shear=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0,F.windP[0].y)*(h*h*0.13*F.weather.w);
      let q=(world+shear)*scale;
      let lod=max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*max(scale.x,scale.y)*128.0),0.0);
      let shape=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q,lod);
      let base=clamp((shape.r-0.5)*2.8+0.68-(1.0-shape.g)*0.12,0.0,1.0);
      let probability=cover*profile;
      var density=clamp((base-(1.0-probability))/max(probability,0.05),0.0,1.0);
      if(strat<0.5){
        let thermal=thermalShape(world,altitude,cover,storm);
        let billow=(shape.r-0.5)*3.4+(shape.g-0.4)*0.65;
        density=clamp((thermal+billow)*2.0,0.0,1.0)*smoothstep(-0.32,0.06,thermal);
        // Storm banks merge into a connected lower deck as coverage approaches one.
        let deck=clamp((base-(1.0-cover*profile))*2.6,0.0,1.0);
        density=max(density,deck*storm*smoothstep(0.65,0.96,cover));
      }
      if(density>0.0){
        let erosion=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q*2.83+vec3f(0.17,0.31,0.59),lod+1.5).b;
        let carve=(1.0-erosion)*mix(0.42,0.07,strat)*mix(1.3,0.65,smoothstep(0.0,0.3,h));
        density=clamp((density-carve)/(1.0-carve),0.0,1.0);
      }
      return density;
    }
    fn cloudDensity(p:vec3f,detail:bool)->f32{return cloudDensityFiltered(p,detail,0.0);}
    fn cloudShadowFor(p: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> f32 {
      if(light.y<0.01){return 1.0;}
      let start=max(0.0,(F.cloudP.x-p.y)/light.y);
      let lengthRay=min(30.0,F.weather.w/max(light.y,0.03));
      var od=0.0;
      for(var j=0u;j<12u;j++) {
        od+=cloudDensity(p+light*(start+(f32(j)+0.5)*lengthRay/12.0),true)*lengthRay/12.0;
      }
      return exp(-od*F.cloudP.z*6.0);
    }
    fn cloudShadowAt(p:vec3f)->f32 {return cloudShadowFor(p,F.sunDir.xyz);}
    fn cloudLayers(dir: vec3f,pixAng:f32,aa:vec2f,sunT:ptr<function,f32>) -> vec4f {
      *sunT=1.0;
      if(F.cloudP.y<0.001 && F.atmo.z<0.001){return vec4f(0);}
      let altitude=max(F.camPos.y*0.001,0.001);
      if(dir.y < -sqrt(2.0*altitude/RG)-0.002){return vec4f(0);}
      let ro=vec3f(0,altitude,0);
      let sphereOrigin=vec3f(0,RG+altitude,0);
      let outer=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w);
      let inner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x);
      var start=max(0.0,outer.x);
      var finish=max(0.0,outer.y);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x){start=max(0.0,inner.y);}
      else if(inner.x>0.0){finish=inner.x;}
      let ground=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG);
      if(ground.x>0.0){finish=min(finish,ground.x);}
      finish=min(finish,200.0);
      // Keep the quadrature topology continuous across image rows. Rounding a
      // view-angle-dependent count rephases every sample at each integer boundary.
      let samples=u32(F.control.x*1.5);
      let logRange=log(max((finish+1.0)/(start+1.0),1.0));
      var trans=1.0;var radiance=vec3f(0);
      let ambient=skyAtmo(vec3f(0,1,0))*0.65+skyAtmo(normalize(vec3f(dir.x,0.25,dir.z)))*0.22;
      let phase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-0.25);
      // Deterministic segment quadrature: no screen-space jitter to crawl in motion.
      // Exponential spacing preserves nearby volume detail and reaches the curved horizon.
      for(var i=0u;i<samples && logRange>0.0;i++) {
        let a=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let b=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i+1u)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let ds=b-a;
        let t=(a+b)*0.5;
        let p=ro+dir*t;
        let footprint=max(ds*0.14,t*pixAng);
        var density=0.0;
        // Four subsegments retained after the cheaper two-point experiment visibly
        // reintroduced distant density slices. No jitter or temporal accumulation.
        for(var sub=0u;sub<4u;sub++){
          density+=cloudDensityFiltered(ro+dir*(a+ds*(f32(sub)+0.5)*0.25),true,footprint)*0.25;
        }
        if(density>0.001) {
          let radial=vec3f(p.x,RG+p.y,p.z);
          let radius=length(radial);let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
          var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
          if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
          var shadow=0.0;
          var lightStep=0.06;
          var lightDist=0.03;
          for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++) {
            shadow+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.sunDir.xyz*lightDist,false,lightStep*0.35)*lightStep;
            lightDist+=lightStep;lightStep*=2.1;
          }
          let sigma=F.cloudP.z*6.0;
          let optical=shadow*sigma;
          let direct=exp(-optical)*phase;
          let multi=(0.6*exp(-optical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-optical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
          let height=clamp((p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG)-F.cloudP.x)/F.weather.w,0.0,1.0);
          var lunar=vec3f(0);
          if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
            let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
            if(!sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu)){
              let moonPhase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),-0.25);
              var moonOptical=0.0;var moonStep=0.06;var moonDist=0.03;
              for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++){
                moonOptical+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.moonDir.xyz*moonDist,false,moonStep*0.35)*moonStep;
                moonDist+=moonStep;moonStep*=2.1;
              }
              moonOptical*=sigma;
              let moonMulti=(0.6*exp(-moonOptical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-moonOptical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
              lunar=sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x*(exp(-moonOptical)*moonPhase+moonMulti);
            }
          }
          let light=ambient*mix(0.18,1.0,sqrt(height))+sun*(direct+multi)+lunar;
          let stepT=exp(-density*sigma*ds);
          let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
          let fog=skyAtmo(dir);
          radiance+=trans*(1.0-stepT)*(light*aerial+fog*(1.0-aerial));
          trans*=stepT;
          if(trans<0.005){break;}
        }
      }
      // Spherical ice shell: finite grazing path, independent of the lower cloud cutoff.
      let iceInner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.0);
      let iceOuter=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.3);
      var iceStart=max(0.0,iceOuter.x);
      var iceFinish=max(0.0,iceOuter.y);
      if(altitude<7.0){iceStart=max(0.0,iceInner.y);}
      else if(iceInner.x>0.0){iceFinish=iceInner.x;}
      if(ground.x>0.0){iceFinish=min(iceFinish,ground.x);}
      if(iceFinish>iceStart && F.atmo.z>0.001) {
        let t=(iceStart+iceFinish)*0.5;
        let p=(ro+dir*t).xz+F.camPos.xz*0.001+F.worldOrigin.xy;
        let warp=vec2f(gnoise(p*0.14),gnoise(p*0.21+vec2f(8.3,1.1)))*2.2;
        let footprint=t*pixAng;
        let low=mix(gnoise((p+warp)*vec2f(0.22,0.85)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*0.85));
        let high=mix(gnoise(p*vec2f(0.63,2.3)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*2.3));
        let ice=max(0.0,0.65*low+0.35*high+F.atmo.z-0.45);
        let opacity=1.0-exp(-ice*0.6*(iceFinish-iceStart));
        let radial=sphereOrigin+dir*t;let radius=length(radial);
        let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
        var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
        if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
        let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
        let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
        let moon=select(sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x,vec3f(0),sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu));
        let iceLight=mix(skyAtmo(dir),ambient+(sun+moon)*0.12,aerial);
        radiance+=trans*opacity*iceLight;
        trans*=1.0-opacity;
      }
      *sunT=trans;
      return vec4f(radiance,1.0-trans);
    }
    fn nightStars(dir:vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let q=vec2f(atan2(dir.x,dir.z)+F.control.w,asin(clamp(dir.y,-1.0,1.0)))*650.0;
      let cell=floor(q);let offset=fract(q)-0.5;
      let h=hash3(vec3f(cell,17.0));
      let stars=smoothstep(0.995,1.0,h)*exp(-dot(offset,offset)*70.0);
      return vec3f(0.65,0.78,1.0)*stars*0.018*F.solarDir.w*smoothstep(0.0,0.2,dir.y);
    }
    fn sunDisk(dir:vec3f)->vec3f {
      let angle=acos(clamp(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-1.0,1.0));
      let pix=max(F.screen.w*length(F.camUp.xyz),0.00002);
      let disk=1.0-smoothstep(SUN_ANG_R-pix,SUN_ANG_R+pix,angle);
      let vis=step(-0.006,dir.y);
      var radiance=disk*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.sunDir.w/(PI*SUN_ANG_R*SUN_ANG_R);
      if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
        let radius=F.celestial.y;
        let tangent=dir-F.moonDir.xyz*dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz);
        let r2=dot(tangent,tangent)/(radius*radius);
        let moonMask=1.0-smoothstep(radius-pix,radius+pix,length(tangent));
        let normal=normalize(tangent/radius-F.moonDir.xyz*sqrt(max(0.0,1.0-r2)));
        let illumination=max(dot(normal,F.solarDir.xyz),0.0);
        let normalization=max((2.0/3.0)*F.celestial.z,1e-7);
        radiance+=moonMask*step(0.0,dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz))*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.celestial.x*illumination/(PI*radius*radius*normalization);
      }
      return radiance;
    }
    fn skyRadiance(dirIn:vec3f,withSun:bool,pixAng:f32)->vec3f {
      let dir=normalize(dirIn);
      var tr=1.0;
      let cl=cloudLayers(dir,pixAng,vec2f(0),&tr);
      var L=skyAtmo(dir)*(1.0-cl.a)+cl.rgb+nightStars(dir)*tr;
      if(withSun){L+=sunDisk(dir)*tr;}
      return L;
    }
    
    @compute @workgroup_size(8,8) fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3u) {
     let dims=textureDimensions(dst);if(any(id.xy>=dims)){return;}
     let uv=(vec2f(id.xy)+0.5)/vec2f(dims);
     let p=vec3f((uv.x-0.5)*40.0,0.0,(uv.y-0.5)*40.0);
     var lunar=1.0;if(F.celestial.x>0.0){lunar=cloudShadowFor(p,F.moonDir.xyz);}
     textureStore(dst,id.xy,vec4f(cloudShadowAt(p),lunar,0,1));
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(0) var src: texture_2d_array<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(1) var dst: texture_storage_2d_array<rgba16float, write>;
    @compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)
    fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id: vec3u) {
      let size = textureDimensions(dst);
      if (id.x >= size.x || id.y >= size.y) { return; }
      let srcSize = vec2i(textureDimensions(src));
      let p = vec2i(id.xy) * 2;
      // 4x4 tent-ish filter for smoother roughness blur
      var acc = vec4f(0.0);
      var wsum = 0.0;
      for (var j = -1; j <= 2; j++) {
        for (var i = -1; i <= 2; i++) {
          let q = clamp(p + vec2i(i, j), vec2i(0), srcSize - 1);
          let w = select(1.0, 3.0, i == 0 || i == 1) * select(1.0, 3.0, j == 0 || j == 1);
          acc += textureLoad(src, q, id.z, 0) * w;
          wsum += w;
        }
      }
      textureStore(dst, id.xy, id.z, acc / wsum);
    }
    
    
    struct Frame {
      viewProj: mat4x4f,   // camera-relative view-projection (reverse-Z, infinite)
      camPos:   vec4f,     // xyz metres, w = time (s)
      camRight: vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovX/2)
      camUp:    vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovY/2)
      camFwd:   vec4f,     // xyz
      sunDir:   vec4f,     // xyz towards sun, w = solar illuminance scale
      screen:   vec4f,     // w, h, 1/w, 1/h
      cascL:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // cascade patch sizes (m), index [c>>2][c&3]
      cascInvL: array<vec4f, 2>,
      windP:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // [0].xy = direction, [0].z = m/s; [1].x = HDR storage divisor
      lodP:     vec4f,     // CDLOD base range, grid quads per node, -, -
      post:     vec4f,     // exposure, bloom strength, frame index, -
      atmo:     vec4f,     // haze (mie multiplier), camera altitude km, -, -
      waterCol: vec4f,     // body scatter colour rgb, unresolved slope variance
      sssCol:   vec4f,     // sss colour rgb, sss strength
      foamP:    vec4f,     // J threshold, J gain, foam intensity, lace sharpness
      fogExt:   vec4f,     // rgb extinction per metre near sea level
      cloudP:   vec4f,     // altitude km, coverage, optical density, wind time
      absorption: vec4f, // RGB proxy wavelengths 650/550/450 nm, 1/m; water height at camera
      backscatter: vec4f, // particulate + molecular backscatter, 1/m; dt
      moonDir: vec4f,     // direction; illuminated fraction
      solarDir: vec4f,    // astronomical sun; night blend
      weather: vec4f,     // rain, spray, cloud type, cloud thickness km
      control: vec4f,     // cloud samples, light samples, underwater mode, star rotation
      celestial: vec4f,   // lunar irradiance, lunar angular radius, Lambert phase, exposure adaptation
      worldOrigin: vec4f, // global xz kilometres; this frame's local-origin shift xz metres
      waveOrigins: array<vec4f,3>, // two modulo cascade UV origins per vec4
      patternOrigins: array<vec4f,2>, // active/residual foam; streak/bubble UV origins
    };
    
    const PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979;
    const RG: f32 = 6360.0;
    const RT: f32 = 6460.0;
    const RAY_S: vec3f = vec3f(5.802e-3, 13.558e-3, 33.1e-3);
    const MIE_S: f32 = 3.996e-3;
    const MIE_E: f32 = 4.40e-3;
    const OZO_A: vec3f = vec3f(0.650e-3, 1.881e-3, 0.085e-3);
    const SUN_ANG_R: f32 = 0.004675;
    const TRANS_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const TRANS_H: f32 = 64.0;
    const MS_SIZE: f32 = 32.0;
    
    struct Medium { ext: vec3f, rayS: vec3f, mieS: f32 };
    
    fn medium(h: f32, haze: f32) -> Medium {
      let hh = max(h, 0.0);
      let dR = exp(-hh / 8.0);
      // aerosols: regular 1.2 km layer plus a dense marine boundary layer
      let dM = exp(-hh / 1.2);
      let dO = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(hh - 25.0) / 15.0);
      var m: Medium;
      m.rayS = RAY_S * dR;
      m.mieS = MIE_S * dM * haze;
      m.ext = m.rayS + vec3f(MIE_E * dM * haze) + OZO_A * dO;
      return m;
    }
    
    fn raySphere(ro: vec3f, rd: vec3f, R: f32) -> vec2f {
      let l = length(ro);
      let b = dot(ro, rd);
      let c = (l - R) * (l + R);
      let d = b * b - c;
      if (d < 0.0) { return vec2f(-1.0, -1.0); }
      let s = sqrt(d);
      return vec2f(-b - s, -b + s);
    }
    
    fn rhoOf(r: f32) -> f32 { return sqrt(max(0.0, (r - RG) * (r + RG))); }
    
    fn transRMuToUV(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = rhoOf(r);
      let disc = r * r * (mu * mu - 1.0) + RT * RT;
      let d = max(0.0, -r * mu + sqrt(max(disc, 0.0)));
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let x = (d - dMin) / (dMax - dMin);
      let y = rho / H;
      return vec2f(x, y);
    }
    
    fn transUVToRMu(uv: vec2f) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = H * uv.y;
      let r = sqrt(rho * rho + RG * RG);
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let d = dMin + uv.x * (dMax - dMin);
      var mu = 1.0;
      if (d > 0.0) { mu = (H * H - rho * rho - d * d) / (2.0 * r * d); }
      return vec2f(r, clamp(mu, -1.0, 1.0));
    }
    
    fn fromUnitToSubUvs(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u + 0.5 / res) * (res / (res + 1.0)); }
    fn fromSubUvsToUnit(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u - 0.5 / res) * (res / (res - 1.0)); }
    
    fn phaseRayleigh(c: f32) -> f32 { return 3.0 / (16.0 * PI) * (1.0 + c * c); }
    fn phaseMie(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return 3.0 / (8.0 * PI) * ((1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + c * c)) / ((2.0 + g2) * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    fn phaseHG(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * PI * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    
    // true when the sun is below the geometric horizon of a point at radius r
    fn sunBelowHorizon(r: f32, mu: f32) -> bool {
      return mu < -rhoOf(r) / r;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> F: Frame;
    @group(0) @binding(1) var transLUT: texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(2) var skyLUT: texture_2d_array<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(3) var sampC: sampler;
    @group(0) @binding(4) var dst: texture_storage_2d_array<rgba16float, write>;
    @group(0) @binding(7) var diffuseCube:texture_cube<f32>;
    
    fn pcgHash(v: u32) -> u32 {
      let s = v * 747796405u + 2891336453u;
      let w = ((s >> ((s >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ s) * 277803737u;
      return (w >> 22u) ^ w;
    }
    fn hashGrad(i: vec2i) -> vec2f {
      let h = pcgHash(u32(i.x) * 1664525u + pcgHash(u32(i.y) + 1013904223u));
      let a = f32(h) * (6.28318530718 / 4294967296.0);
      return vec2f(cos(a), sin(a));
    }
    fn gnoise(p: vec2f) -> f32 {
      let i = vec2i(floor(p));
      let f = p - floor(p);
      let u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);
      let a = dot(hashGrad(i), f);
      let b = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 0)), f - vec2f(1.0, 0.0));
      let c = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(0, 1)), f - vec2f(0.0, 1.0));
      let d = dot(hashGrad(i + vec2i(1, 1)), f - vec2f(1.0, 1.0));
      return mix(mix(a, b, u.x), mix(c, d, u.x), u.y);
    }
    
    const SKY_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const SKY_H: f32 = 144.0;
    
    fn camR() -> f32 { return RG + F.atmo.y; }
    
    fn sampleTrans(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec3f {
      return textureSampleLevel(transLUT, sampC, transRMuToUV(r, mu), 0.0).rgb;
    }
    
    fn sunIlluminance() -> vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.sunDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(), F.sunDir.y) * F.sunDir.w;
    }
    fn moonIlluminance()->vec3f {
      if(sunBelowHorizon(camR(),F.moonDir.y)){return vec3f(0);}
      return sampleTrans(camR(),F.moonDir.y)*F.celestial.x;
    }
    
    fn skyViewUV(dir: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> vec2f {
      let r = camR();
      let vHorizon = rhoOf(r);
      let cosBeta = vHorizon / r;
      let beta = acos(clamp(cosBeta, -1.0, 1.0));
      let zha = PI - beta;
      let vzc = clamp(dir.y, -1.0, 1.0);
      var uv: vec2f;
      if (vzc >= -cosBeta) {
        var coord = acos(vzc) / zha;
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        uv.y = coord * 0.5;
      } else {
        var coord = (acos(vzc) - zha) / beta;
        coord = sqrt(max(coord, 0.0));
        uv.y = coord * 0.5 + 0.5;
      }
      let dl = length(dir.xz);
      let sl = length(light.xz);
      var lvc = 1.0;
      if (dl > 1e-6 && sl > 1e-6) { lvc = clamp(dot(dir.xz, light.xz) / (dl * sl), -1.0, 1.0); }
      uv.x = sqrt(max(-lvc * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0));
      return vec2f(fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.x, SKY_W), fromUnitToSubUvs(uv.y, SKY_H));
    }
    
    fn skyAtmo(dir: vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let solar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0u,0.0).rgb*F.sunDir.w;
      let lunar=textureSampleLevel(skyLUT,sampC,skyViewUV(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),1u,0.0).rgb*F.celestial.x;
      return solar+lunar;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(5) var cloudNoise:texture_3d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(6) var cloudNoiseSampler:sampler;
    fn hash3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      var q=fract(p*0.1031);
      q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);
      return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);
    }
    fn noise3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      let i=floor(p); let f=fract(p); let u=f*f*(3.0-2.0*f);
      return mix(mix(mix(hash3(i),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,0)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,0)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,0)),u.x),u.y),
        mix(mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,0,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,0,1)),u.x),mix(hash3(i+vec3f(0,1,1)),hash3(i+vec3f(1,1,1)),u.x),u.y),u.z);
    }
    fn filteredNoise(p:vec3f, footprint:f32)->f32 {
      return mix(noise3(p),0.5,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint));
    }
    fn thermalShape(world:vec3f,altitude:f32,coverage:f32,storm:f32)->f32 {
      let size=mix(3.6,7.0,storm);
      let cell=floor(world.xz/size);
      var field=-1.0;
      for(var j=-1;j<=1;j++){for(var i=-1;i<=1;i++){
        let id=cell+vec2f(f32(i),f32(j));
        let random=vec3f(hash3(vec3f(id,13.7)),hash3(vec3f(id.yx,29.3)),hash3(vec3f(id,71.9)));
        let strength=smoothstep(random.z-0.12,random.z+0.12,coverage);
        if(strength<0.01){continue;}
        let centre=(id+0.2+random.xy*0.6)*size;
        let depth=F.weather.w*(0.20+0.64*random.y*random.y);
        let bottom=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.015+0.08*random.x);
        let h=(altitude-bottom)/max(depth,0.15);
        let radius=size*(0.17+0.29*random.x)*pow(strength,0.333333);
        let bend=F.windP[0].xy*h*h*depth*0.18;
        let q=vec3f((world.xz-centre-bend)/radius,(h-0.30)/0.52);
        let core=1.0-length(q);
        let turret=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy-(random.xy-0.5)*0.95)/(0.44+random.x*0.25),(h-0.52)/0.44));
        let side=1.0-length(vec3f((q.xy+(random.yx-0.5)*1.4)/0.62,(h-0.31)/0.39));
        let lobe=max(max(core,turret),side);
        let base=smoothstep(-0.01,0.055,h);
        field=max(field,mix(-1.0,lobe,base));
      }}
      return field;
    }
    fn cloudDensityFiltered(p: vec3f, detail: bool, footprint:f32) -> f32 {
      let altitude=p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x || altitude>F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w){return 0.0;}
      let drift=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0.0,F.windP[0].y)*F.cloudP.w*F.windP[0].z*0.00065;
      let world=p+vec3f(F.camPos.x*0.001+F.worldOrigin.x,0,F.camPos.z*0.001+F.worldOrigin.y)-drift;
      let strat=select(0.0,1.0,F.weather.z>0.5 && F.weather.z<1.5);
      let storm=step(1.5,F.weather.z);
      // Separate mesoscale organisation from billows. Local depth and cloud base
      // vary across each bank instead of sharing one repeated horizontal profile.
      let weather=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.008,0.31,world.z*0.008),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*1.024),0.0));
      let cell=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,vec3f(world.x*0.047,0.67,world.z*0.047),max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*6.016),0.0));
      let cover=clamp(F.cloudP.y+(weather.a-0.5)*3.2+(cell.a-0.5)*0.65,0.0,1.0);
      let baseHeight=F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w*(0.025+0.14*cell.a);
      let depth=F.weather.w*mix(0.48+0.48*weather.r,0.22+0.15*cell.r,strat);
      let h=(altitude-baseHeight)/max(depth,0.1);
      if((strat>0.5 && (h<0.0 || h>1.0)) || cover<0.001){return 0.0;}
      let profile=smoothstep(0.0,0.075,h)*(1.0-smoothstep(mix(0.6,0.7,storm),1.0,h));
      let scale=vec3f(mix(0.62,0.24,storm),mix(0.60,0.42,strat),mix(0.62,0.24,storm));
      let shear=vec3f(F.windP[0].x,0,F.windP[0].y)*(h*h*0.13*F.weather.w);
      let q=(world+shear)*scale;
      let lod=max(log2(max(footprint,0.001)*max(scale.x,scale.y)*128.0),0.0);
      let shape=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q,lod);
      let base=clamp((shape.r-0.5)*2.8+0.68-(1.0-shape.g)*0.12,0.0,1.0);
      let probability=cover*profile;
      var density=clamp((base-(1.0-probability))/max(probability,0.05),0.0,1.0);
      if(strat<0.5){
        let thermal=thermalShape(world,altitude,cover,storm);
        let billow=(shape.r-0.5)*3.4+(shape.g-0.4)*0.65;
        density=clamp((thermal+billow)*2.0,0.0,1.0)*smoothstep(-0.32,0.06,thermal);
        // Storm banks merge into a connected lower deck as coverage approaches one.
        let deck=clamp((base-(1.0-cover*profile))*2.6,0.0,1.0);
        density=max(density,deck*storm*smoothstep(0.65,0.96,cover));
      }
      if(density>0.0){
        let erosion=textureSampleLevel(cloudNoise,cloudNoiseSampler,q*2.83+vec3f(0.17,0.31,0.59),lod+1.5).b;
        let carve=(1.0-erosion)*mix(0.42,0.07,strat)*mix(1.3,0.65,smoothstep(0.0,0.3,h));
        density=clamp((density-carve)/(1.0-carve),0.0,1.0);
      }
      return density;
    }
    fn cloudDensity(p:vec3f,detail:bool)->f32{return cloudDensityFiltered(p,detail,0.0);}
    fn cloudShadowFor(p: vec3f,light:vec3f) -> f32 {
      if(light.y<0.01){return 1.0;}
      let start=max(0.0,(F.cloudP.x-p.y)/light.y);
      let lengthRay=min(30.0,F.weather.w/max(light.y,0.03));
      var od=0.0;
      for(var j=0u;j<12u;j++) {
        od+=cloudDensity(p+light*(start+(f32(j)+0.5)*lengthRay/12.0),true)*lengthRay/12.0;
      }
      return exp(-od*F.cloudP.z*6.0);
    }
    fn cloudShadowAt(p:vec3f)->f32 {return cloudShadowFor(p,F.sunDir.xyz);}
    fn cloudLayers(dir: vec3f,pixAng:f32,aa:vec2f,sunT:ptr<function,f32>) -> vec4f {
      *sunT=1.0;
      if(F.cloudP.y<0.001 && F.atmo.z<0.001){return vec4f(0);}
      let altitude=max(F.camPos.y*0.001,0.001);
      if(dir.y < -sqrt(2.0*altitude/RG)-0.002){return vec4f(0);}
      let ro=vec3f(0,altitude,0);
      let sphereOrigin=vec3f(0,RG+altitude,0);
      let outer=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x+F.weather.w);
      let inner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+F.cloudP.x);
      var start=max(0.0,outer.x);
      var finish=max(0.0,outer.y);
      if(altitude<F.cloudP.x){start=max(0.0,inner.y);}
      else if(inner.x>0.0){finish=inner.x;}
      let ground=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG);
      if(ground.x>0.0){finish=min(finish,ground.x);}
      finish=min(finish,200.0);
      // Keep the quadrature topology continuous across image rows. Rounding a
      // view-angle-dependent count rephases every sample at each integer boundary.
      let samples=u32(F.control.x*1.5);
      let logRange=log(max((finish+1.0)/(start+1.0),1.0));
      var trans=1.0;var radiance=vec3f(0);
      let ambient=skyAtmo(vec3f(0,1,0))*0.65+skyAtmo(normalize(vec3f(dir.x,0.25,dir.z)))*0.22;
      let phase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-0.25);
      // Deterministic segment quadrature: no screen-space jitter to crawl in motion.
      // Exponential spacing preserves nearby volume detail and reaches the curved horizon.
      for(var i=0u;i<samples && logRange>0.0;i++) {
        let a=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let b=(start+1.0)*exp(logRange*f32(i+1u)/f32(samples))-1.0;
        let ds=b-a;
        let t=(a+b)*0.5;
        let p=ro+dir*t;
        let footprint=max(ds*0.14,t*pixAng);
        var density=0.0;
        // Four subsegments retained after the cheaper two-point experiment visibly
        // reintroduced distant density slices. No jitter or temporal accumulation.
        for(var sub=0u;sub<4u;sub++){
          density+=cloudDensityFiltered(ro+dir*(a+ds*(f32(sub)+0.5)*0.25),true,footprint)*0.25;
        }
        if(density>0.001) {
          let radial=vec3f(p.x,RG+p.y,p.z);
          let radius=length(radial);let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
          var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
          if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
          var shadow=0.0;
          var lightStep=0.06;
          var lightDist=0.03;
          for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++) {
            shadow+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.sunDir.xyz*lightDist,false,lightStep*0.35)*lightStep;
            lightDist+=lightStep;lightStep*=2.1;
          }
          let sigma=F.cloudP.z*6.0;
          let optical=shadow*sigma;
          let direct=exp(-optical)*phase;
          let multi=(0.6*exp(-optical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-optical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
          let height=clamp((p.y+dot(p.xz,p.xz)/(2.0*RG)-F.cloudP.x)/F.weather.w,0.0,1.0);
          var lunar=vec3f(0);
          if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
            let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
            if(!sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu)){
              let moonPhase=0.85*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.68)+0.15*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),-0.25);
              var moonOptical=0.0;var moonStep=0.06;var moonDist=0.03;
              for(var j=0u;j<u32(F.control.y);j++){
                moonOptical+=cloudDensityFiltered(p+F.moonDir.xyz*moonDist,false,moonStep*0.35)*moonStep;
                moonDist+=moonStep;moonStep*=2.1;
              }
              moonOptical*=sigma;
              let moonMulti=(0.6*exp(-moonOptical*0.25)*phaseHG(dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz),0.28)+0.3*exp(-moonOptical*0.07)/(4.0*PI))*(1.0-exp(-density*5.0));
              lunar=sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x*(exp(-moonOptical)*moonPhase+moonMulti);
            }
          }
          let light=ambient*mix(0.18,1.0,sqrt(height))+sun*(direct+multi)+lunar;
          let stepT=exp(-density*sigma*ds);
          let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
          let fog=skyAtmo(dir);
          radiance+=trans*(1.0-stepT)*(light*aerial+fog*(1.0-aerial));
          trans*=stepT;
          if(trans<0.005){break;}
        }
      }
      // Spherical ice shell: finite grazing path, independent of the lower cloud cutoff.
      let iceInner=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.0);
      let iceOuter=raySphere(sphereOrigin,dir,RG+7.3);
      var iceStart=max(0.0,iceOuter.x);
      var iceFinish=max(0.0,iceOuter.y);
      if(altitude<7.0){iceStart=max(0.0,iceInner.y);}
      else if(iceInner.x>0.0){iceFinish=iceInner.x;}
      if(ground.x>0.0){iceFinish=min(iceFinish,ground.x);}
      if(iceFinish>iceStart && F.atmo.z>0.001) {
        let t=(iceStart+iceFinish)*0.5;
        let p=(ro+dir*t).xz+F.camPos.xz*0.001+F.worldOrigin.xy;
        let warp=vec2f(gnoise(p*0.14),gnoise(p*0.21+vec2f(8.3,1.1)))*2.2;
        let footprint=t*pixAng;
        let low=mix(gnoise((p+warp)*vec2f(0.22,0.85)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*0.85));
        let high=mix(gnoise(p*vec2f(0.63,2.3)),0.0,smoothstep(0.25,0.85,footprint*2.3));
        let ice=max(0.0,0.65*low+0.35*high+F.atmo.z-0.45);
        let opacity=1.0-exp(-ice*0.6*(iceFinish-iceStart));
        let radial=sphereOrigin+dir*t;let radius=length(radial);
        let mu=dot(radial,F.sunDir.xyz)/radius;
        var sun=sampleTrans(radius,mu)*F.sunDir.w;
        if(sunBelowHorizon(radius,mu)){sun=vec3f(0);}
        let aerial=exp(-t*(0.008+F.atmo.x*0.003));
        let moonMu=dot(radial,F.moonDir.xyz)/radius;
        let moon=select(sampleTrans(radius,moonMu)*F.celestial.x,vec3f(0),sunBelowHorizon(radius,moonMu));
        let iceLight=mix(skyAtmo(dir),ambient+(sun+moon)*0.12,aerial);
        radiance+=trans*opacity*iceLight;
        trans*=1.0-opacity;
      }
      *sunT=trans;
      return vec4f(radiance,1.0-trans);
    }
    fn nightStars(dir:vec3f) -> vec3f {
      let q=vec2f(atan2(dir.x,dir.z)+F.control.w,asin(clamp(dir.y,-1.0,1.0)))*650.0;
      let cell=floor(q);let offset=fract(q)-0.5;
      let h=hash3(vec3f(cell,17.0));
      let stars=smoothstep(0.995,1.0,h)*exp(-dot(offset,offset)*70.0);
      return vec3f(0.65,0.78,1.0)*stars*0.018*F.solarDir.w*smoothstep(0.0,0.2,dir.y);
    }
    fn sunDisk(dir:vec3f)->vec3f {
      let angle=acos(clamp(dot(dir,F.sunDir.xyz),-1.0,1.0));
      let pix=max(F.screen.w*length(F.camUp.xyz),0.00002);
      let disk=1.0-smoothstep(SUN_ANG_R-pix,SUN_ANG_R+pix,angle);
      let vis=step(-0.006,dir.y);
      var radiance=disk*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.sunDir.w/(PI*SUN_ANG_R*SUN_ANG_R);
      if(F.celestial.x>0.0){
        let radius=F.celestial.y;
        let tangent=dir-F.moonDir.xyz*dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz);
        let r2=dot(tangent,tangent)/(radius*radius);
        let moonMask=1.0-smoothstep(radius-pix,radius+pix,length(tangent));
        let normal=normalize(tangent/radius-F.moonDir.xyz*sqrt(max(0.0,1.0-r2)));
        let illumination=max(dot(normal,F.solarDir.xyz),0.0);
        let normalization=max((2.0/3.0)*F.celestial.z,1e-7);
        radiance+=moonMask*step(0.0,dot(dir,F.moonDir.xyz))*vis*sampleTrans(camR(),max(dir.y,-0.01))*F.celestial.x*illumination/(PI*radius*radius*normalization);
      }
      return radiance;
    }
    fn skyRadiance(dirIn:vec3f,withSun:bool,pixAng:f32)->vec3f {
      let dir=normalize(dirIn);
      var tr=1.0;
      let cl=cloudLayers(dir,pixAng,vec2f(0),&tr);
      var L=skyAtmo(dir)*(1.0-cl.a)+cl.rgb+nightStars(dir)*tr;
      if(withSun){L+=sunDisk(dir)*tr;}
      return L;
    }
    
    const ENV: f32 = 128.0;
    fn faceDir(face: u32, uv: vec2f) -> vec3f {
      let u = uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0;
      let v = uv.y * 2.0 - 1.0;
      switch (face) {
        case 0u: { return vec3f(1.0, -v, -u); }
        case 1u: { return vec3f(-1.0, -v, u); }
        case 2u: { return vec3f(u, 1.0, v); }
        case 3u: { return vec3f(u, -1.0, -v); }
        case 4u: { return vec3f(u, -v, 1.0); }
        default: { return vec3f(-u, -v, -1.0); }
      }
    }
    @compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)
    fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id: vec3u) {
      if (f32(id.x) >= ENV || f32(id.y) >= ENV) { return; }
      // faces are refreshed round-robin: face = (frame + z) % 6
      let face = (u32(F.post.z) + id.z) % 6u;
      let dir = normalize(faceDir(face, (vec2f(id.xy) + 0.5) / ENV));
      var L: vec3f;
      if (dir.y >= 0.0) {
        L = skyRadiance(dir, false, 1.5708 / ENV * 1.5);
      } else {
        // First-bounce homogeneous sea approximation. Same dielectric and deep-water
        // radiometry as the surface, using the preceding irradiance update.
        let ci=max(-dir.y,0.00001);
        let ct=sqrt(1.0-(1.0-ci*ci)/(1.333*1.333));
        let rs=(ci-1.333*ct)/(ci+1.333*ct);let rp=(1.333*ci-ct)/(1.333*ci+ct);
        let fresnel=0.5*(rs*rs+rp*rp);
        let reflected=skyRadiance(vec3f(dir.x,-dir.y,dir.z),false,1.5708/ENV*1.5);
        let ratio=F.backscatter.rgb/(F.absorption.rgb+F.backscatter.rgb);
        let incoming=textureSampleLevel(diffuseCube,sampC,vec3f(0,1,0),0.0).rgb*0.94+(sunIlluminance()*max(F.sunDir.y,0.0)+moonIlluminance()*max(F.moonDir.y,0.0))*0.96;
        let body=(0.0949*ratio+0.0794*ratio*ratio)*incoming/(1.333*1.333);
        L=reflected*fresnel+body*(1.0-fresnel);
      }
      textureStore(dst, id.xy, face, vec4f(L, 1.0));
    }
    
    
    const PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979;
    const RG: f32 = 6360.0;
    const RT: f32 = 6460.0;
    const RAY_S: vec3f = vec3f(5.802e-3, 13.558e-3, 33.1e-3);
    const MIE_S: f32 = 3.996e-3;
    const MIE_E: f32 = 4.40e-3;
    const OZO_A: vec3f = vec3f(0.650e-3, 1.881e-3, 0.085e-3);
    const SUN_ANG_R: f32 = 0.004675;
    const TRANS_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const TRANS_H: f32 = 64.0;
    const MS_SIZE: f32 = 32.0;
    
    struct Medium { ext: vec3f, rayS: vec3f, mieS: f32 };
    
    fn medium(h: f32, haze: f32) -> Medium {
      let hh = max(h, 0.0);
      let dR = exp(-hh / 8.0);
      // aerosols: regular 1.2 km layer plus a dense marine boundary layer
      let dM = exp(-hh / 1.2);
      let dO = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(hh - 25.0) / 15.0);
      var m: Medium;
      m.rayS = RAY_S * dR;
      m.mieS = MIE_S * dM * haze;
      m.ext = m.rayS + vec3f(MIE_E * dM * haze) + OZO_A * dO;
      return m;
    }
    
    fn raySphere(ro: vec3f, rd: vec3f, R: f32) -> vec2f {
      let l = length(ro);
      let b = dot(ro, rd);
      let c = (l - R) * (l + R);
      let d = b * b - c;
      if (d < 0.0) { return vec2f(-1.0, -1.0); }
      let s = sqrt(d);
      return vec2f(-b - s, -b + s);
    }
    
    fn rhoOf(r: f32) -> f32 { return sqrt(max(0.0, (r - RG) * (r + RG))); }
    
    fn transRMuToUV(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = rhoOf(r);
      let disc = r * r * (mu * mu - 1.0) + RT * RT;
      let d = max(0.0, -r * mu + sqrt(max(disc, 0.0)));
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let x = (d - dMin) / (dMax - dMin);
      let y = rho / H;
      return vec2f(x, y);
    }
    
    fn transUVToRMu(uv: vec2f) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = H * uv.y;
      let r = sqrt(rho * rho + RG * RG);
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let d = dMin + uv.x * (dMax - dMin);
      var mu = 1.0;
      if (d > 0.0) { mu = (H * H - rho * rho - d * d) / (2.0 * r * d); }
      return vec2f(r, clamp(mu, -1.0, 1.0));
    }
    
    fn fromUnitToSubUvs(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u + 0.5 / res) * (res / (res + 1.0)); }
    fn fromSubUvsToUnit(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u - 0.5 / res) * (res / (res - 1.0)); }
    
    fn phaseRayleigh(c: f32) -> f32 { return 3.0 / (16.0 * PI) * (1.0 + c * c); }
    fn phaseMie(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return 3.0 / (8.0 * PI) * ((1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + c * c)) / ((2.0 + g2) * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    fn phaseHG(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * PI * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    
    // true when the sun is below the geometric horizon of a point at radius r
    fn sunBelowHorizon(r: f32, mu: f32) -> bool {
      return mu < -rhoOf(r) / r;
    }
    
    struct AtmoP { haze: vec4f };   // x = mie multiplier
    @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> A: AtmoP;
    
    @group(0) @binding(1) var transLUT: texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(2) var sampC: sampler;
    @group(0) @binding(3) var dst: texture_storage_2d<rgba16float, write>;
    
    fn sampleTrans(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec3f {
      return textureSampleLevel(transLUT, sampC, transRMuToUV(r, mu), 0.0).rgb;
    }
    
    @compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)
    fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id: vec3u) {
      if (f32(id.x) >= MS_SIZE || f32(id.y) >= MS_SIZE) { return; }
      var uv = (vec2f(id.xy) + 0.5) / MS_SIZE;
      uv = vec2f(fromSubUvsToUnit(uv.x, MS_SIZE), fromSubUvsToUnit(uv.y, MS_SIZE));
      let cosSun = uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0;
      let h = max(uv.y * (RT - RG), 0.01);
      let r = RG + h;
      let ro = vec3f(0.0, r, 0.0);
      let sun = vec3f(sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - cosSun * cosSun)), cosSun, 0.0);
      var Lsum = vec3f(0.0);
      var Fsum = vec3f(0.0);
      let SQ = 8u;
      let STEPS = 20u;
      for (var a = 0u; a < SQ; a++) {
        for (var b = 0u; b < SQ; b++) {
          let th = acos(1.0 - 2.0 * (f32(b) + 0.5) / f32(SQ));
          let ph = 2.0 * PI * (f32(a) + 0.5) / f32(SQ);
          let rd = vec3f(sin(th) * cos(ph), cos(th), sin(th) * sin(ph));
          let tTop = raySphere(ro, rd, RT);
          let tGr = raySphere(ro, rd, RG);
          var tMax = tTop.y;
          let hitG = tGr.x > 0.0;
          if (hitG) { tMax = tGr.x; }
          let dt = tMax / f32(STEPS);
          var T = vec3f(1.0);
          var L = vec3f(0.0);
          var Fms = vec3f(0.0);
          for (var s = 0u; s < STEPS; s++) {
            let p = ro + rd * ((f32(s) + 0.5) * dt);
            let pr = length(p);
            let m = medium(pr - RG, A.haze.x);
            let scat = m.rayS + vec3f(m.mieS);
            let Ts = exp(-m.ext * dt);
            let smu = dot(p, sun) / pr;
            var sunT = sampleTrans(pr, smu);
            if (sunBelowHorizon(pr, smu)) { sunT = vec3f(0.0); }
            let ext = max(m.ext, vec3f(1e-9));
            let S = scat * sunT / (4.0 * PI);
            L += T * (S - S * Ts) / ext;
            Fms += T * (scat - scat * Ts) / ext;
            T *= Ts;
          }
          if (hitG) {
            let pg = ro + rd * tMax;
            let ng = normalize(pg);
            let smu = dot(ng, sun);
            L += T * sampleTrans(RG, smu) * max(smu, 0.0) * 0.06 / PI;
          }
          Lsum += L;
          Fsum += Fms;
        }
      }
      let invN = 1.0 / f32(SQ * SQ);
      let L2 = Lsum * invN;
      let fms = Fsum * invN;
      textureStore(dst, id.xy, vec4f(L2 / (1.0 - fms), 1.0));
    }
    
    
    struct Frame {
      viewProj: mat4x4f,   // camera-relative view-projection (reverse-Z, infinite)
      camPos:   vec4f,     // xyz metres, w = time (s)
      camRight: vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovX/2)
      camUp:    vec4f,     // xyz * tan(fovY/2)
      camFwd:   vec4f,     // xyz
      sunDir:   vec4f,     // xyz towards sun, w = solar illuminance scale
      screen:   vec4f,     // w, h, 1/w, 1/h
      cascL:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // cascade patch sizes (m), index [c>>2][c&3]
      cascInvL: array<vec4f, 2>,
      windP:    array<vec4f, 2>,  // [0].xy = direction, [0].z = m/s; [1].x = HDR storage divisor
      lodP:     vec4f,     // CDLOD base range, grid quads per node, -, -
      post:     vec4f,     // exposure, bloom strength, frame index, -
      atmo:     vec4f,     // haze (mie multiplier), camera altitude km, -, -
      waterCol: vec4f,     // body scatter colour rgb, unresolved slope variance
      sssCol:   vec4f,     // sss colour rgb, sss strength
      foamP:    vec4f,     // J threshold, J gain, foam intensity, lace sharpness
      fogExt:   vec4f,     // rgb extinction per metre near sea level
      cloudP:   vec4f,     // altitude km, coverage, optical density, wind time
      absorption: vec4f, // RGB proxy wavelengths 650/550/450 nm, 1/m; water height at camera
      backscatter: vec4f, // particulate + molecular backscatter, 1/m; dt
      moonDir: vec4f,     // direction; illuminated fraction
      solarDir: vec4f,    // astronomical sun; night blend
      weather: vec4f,     // rain, spray, cloud type, cloud thickness km
      control: vec4f,     // cloud samples, light samples, underwater mode, star rotation
      celestial: vec4f,   // lunar irradiance, lunar angular radius, Lambert phase, exposure adaptation
      worldOrigin: vec4f, // global xz kilometres; this frame's local-origin shift xz metres
      waveOrigins: array<vec4f,3>, // two modulo cascade UV origins per vec4
      patternOrigins: array<vec4f,2>, // active/residual foam; streak/bubble UV origins
    };
    
    const PI: f32 = 3.14159265358979;
    const RG: f32 = 6360.0;
    const RT: f32 = 6460.0;
    const RAY_S: vec3f = vec3f(5.802e-3, 13.558e-3, 33.1e-3);
    const MIE_S: f32 = 3.996e-3;
    const MIE_E: f32 = 4.40e-3;
    const OZO_A: vec3f = vec3f(0.650e-3, 1.881e-3, 0.085e-3);
    const SUN_ANG_R: f32 = 0.004675;
    const TRANS_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const TRANS_H: f32 = 64.0;
    const MS_SIZE: f32 = 32.0;
    
    struct Medium { ext: vec3f, rayS: vec3f, mieS: f32 };
    
    fn medium(h: f32, haze: f32) -> Medium {
      let hh = max(h, 0.0);
      let dR = exp(-hh / 8.0);
      // aerosols: regular 1.2 km layer plus a dense marine boundary layer
      let dM = exp(-hh / 1.2);
      let dO = max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(hh - 25.0) / 15.0);
      var m: Medium;
      m.rayS = RAY_S * dR;
      m.mieS = MIE_S * dM * haze;
      m.ext = m.rayS + vec3f(MIE_E * dM * haze) + OZO_A * dO;
      return m;
    }
    
    fn raySphere(ro: vec3f, rd: vec3f, R: f32) -> vec2f {
      let l = length(ro);
      let b = dot(ro, rd);
      let c = (l - R) * (l + R);
      let d = b * b - c;
      if (d < 0.0) { return vec2f(-1.0, -1.0); }
      let s = sqrt(d);
      return vec2f(-b - s, -b + s);
    }
    
    fn rhoOf(r: f32) -> f32 { return sqrt(max(0.0, (r - RG) * (r + RG))); }
    
    fn transRMuToUV(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = rhoOf(r);
      let disc = r * r * (mu * mu - 1.0) + RT * RT;
      let d = max(0.0, -r * mu + sqrt(max(disc, 0.0)));
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let x = (d - dMin) / (dMax - dMin);
      let y = rho / H;
      return vec2f(x, y);
    }
    
    fn transUVToRMu(uv: vec2f) -> vec2f {
      let H = sqrt(RT * RT - RG * RG);
      let rho = H * uv.y;
      let r = sqrt(rho * rho + RG * RG);
      let dMin = RT - r;
      let dMax = rho + H;
      let d = dMin + uv.x * (dMax - dMin);
      var mu = 1.0;
      if (d > 0.0) { mu = (H * H - rho * rho - d * d) / (2.0 * r * d); }
      return vec2f(r, clamp(mu, -1.0, 1.0));
    }
    
    fn fromUnitToSubUvs(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u + 0.5 / res) * (res / (res + 1.0)); }
    fn fromSubUvsToUnit(u: f32, res: f32) -> f32 { return (u - 0.5 / res) * (res / (res - 1.0)); }
    
    fn phaseRayleigh(c: f32) -> f32 { return 3.0 / (16.0 * PI) * (1.0 + c * c); }
    fn phaseMie(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return 3.0 / (8.0 * PI) * ((1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + c * c)) / ((2.0 + g2) * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    fn phaseHG(c: f32, g: f32) -> f32 {
      let g2 = g * g;
      return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * PI * pow(max(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * c, 1e-4), 1.5));
    }
    
    // true when the sun is below the geometric horizon of a point at radius r
    fn sunBelowHorizon(r: f32, mu: f32) -> bool {
      return mu < -rhoOf(r) / r;
    }
    
    @group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> F: Frame;
    @group(0) @binding(1) var transLUT: texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(2) var msLUT: texture_2d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(3) var sampC: sampler;
    @group(0) @binding(4) var dst: texture_storage_2d_array<rgba16float, write>;
    const SKY_W: f32 = 256.0;
    const SKY_H: f32 = 144.0;
    
    fn sampleTrans(r: f32, mu: f32) -> vec3f {
      return textureSampleLevel(transLUT, sampC, transRMuToUV(r, mu), 0.0).rgb;
    }
    
    @compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 1)
    fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id: vec3u) {
      if (f32(id.x) >= SKY_W || f32(id.y) >= SKY_H) { return; }
      var uv = (vec2f(id.xy) + 0.5) / vec2f(SKY_W, SKY_H);
      uv = vec2f(fromSubUvsToUnit(uv.x, SKY_W), fromSubUvsToUnit(uv.y, SKY_H));
      let haze = F.atmo.x;
      let r = RG + F.atmo.y;
      let vHorizon = rhoOf(r);
      let cosBeta = vHorizon / r;
      let beta = acos(clamp(cosBeta, -1.0, 1.0));
      let zha = PI - beta;
      var vza: f32;
      if (uv.y < 0.5) {
        var coord = 2.0 * uv.y;
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        coord *= coord;
        coord = 1.0 - coord;
        vza = zha * coord;
      } else {
        var coord = uv.y * 2.0 - 1.0;
        coord *= coord;
        vza = zha + beta * coord;
      }
      let vzc = cos(vza);
      let vzs = sin(vza);
      let cx = uv.x * uv.x;
      let lvc = -(cx * 2.0 - 1.0);
      let lvs = sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - lvc * lvc));
      let rd = vec3f(vzs * lvc, vzc, vzs * lvs);
      let smu = select(F.sunDir.y,F.moonDir.y,id.z==1u);
      let sun = vec3f(sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - smu * smu)), smu, 0.0);
      let ro = vec3f(0.0, r, 0.0);
    
      let tTop = raySphere(ro, rd, RT);
      let tGr = raySphere(ro, rd, RG);
      var tMax = tTop.y;
      if (tGr.x > 0.0) { tMax = tGr.x; }
      let cosT = dot(rd, sun);
      let pR = phaseRayleigh(cosT);
      let pM = phaseMie(cosT, 0.84);
      var L = vec3f(0.0);
      var T = vec3f(1.0);
      let STEPS = 48u;
      var tPrev = 0.0;
      for (var i = 0u; i < STEPS; i++) {
        let fa = (f32(i) + 1.0) / f32(STEPS);
        let tn = tMax * fa * fa;
        let dt = tn - tPrev;
        let t = tPrev + dt * 0.3;
        tPrev = tn;
        let p = ro + rd * t;
        let pr = length(p);
        let h = pr - RG;
        let m = medium(h, haze);
        let pmu = dot(p, sun) / pr;
        var sunT = sampleTrans(pr, pmu);
        if (sunBelowHorizon(pr, pmu)) { sunT = vec3f(0.0); }
        let msuv = vec2f(fromUnitToSubUvs(pmu * 0.5 + 0.5, MS_SIZE), fromUnitToSubUvs(clamp(h / (RT - RG), 0.0, 1.0), MS_SIZE));
        let ms = textureSampleLevel(msLUT, sampC, msuv, 0.0).rgb;
        let S = sunT * (m.rayS * pR + vec3f(m.mieS * pM)) + ms * (m.rayS + vec3f(m.mieS));
        let Ts = exp(-m.ext * dt);
        let ext = max(m.ext, vec3f(1e-9));
        L += T * (S - S * Ts) / ext;
        T *= Ts;
      }
      textureStore(dst, id.xy, id.z, vec4f(L, 1.0));
    }
    
    
    @group(0) @binding(5) var cloudNoise:texture_3d<f32>;
    @group(0) @binding(6) var cloudNoiseSampler:sampler;
    fn hash3(p: vec3f) -> f32 {
      var q=fract(p*0.1031);
      q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);
      return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);
    }
